   60 import Data.Char (ord)

   63 -- | Encode a Haskell String to a list of Word8 values, in UTF8 format.
   64 utf8Encode :: Char -> [Word8]
   65 utf8Encode = uncurry (:) . utf8Encode'
   66 
   67 utf8Encode' :: Char -> (Word8, [Word8])
   68 utf8Encode' c = case go (ord c) of
   69                   (x, xs) -> (fromIntegral x, map fromIntegral xs)
   70  where
   71   go oc
   72    | oc <= 0x7f       = ( oc
   73                         , [
   74                         ])
   75 
   76    | oc <= 0x7ff      = ( 0xc0 + (oc `Data.Bits.shiftR` 6)
   77                         , [0x80 + oc Data.Bits..&. 0x3f
   78                         ])
   79 
   80    | oc <= 0xffff     = ( 0xe0 + (oc `Data.Bits.shiftR` 12)
   81                         , [0x80 + ((oc `Data.Bits.shiftR` 6) Data.Bits..&. 0x3f)
   82                         , 0x80 + oc Data.Bits..&. 0x3f
   83                         ])
   84    | otherwise        = ( 0xf0 + (oc `Data.Bits.shiftR` 18)
   85                         , [0x80 + ((oc `Data.Bits.shiftR` 12) Data.Bits..&. 0x3f)
   86                         , 0x80 + ((oc `Data.Bits.shiftR` 6) Data.Bits..&. 0x3f)
   87                         , 0x80 + oc Data.Bits..&. 0x3f
   88                         ])
   89 
   90 #endif
   91 
   92 type Byte = Word8
   93 
   94 -- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   95 -- The input type
   96 
   97 #if defined(ALEX_POSN) || defined(ALEX_MONAD) || defined(ALEX_GSCAN)
   98 type AlexInput = (AlexPosn,     -- current position,
   99                   Char,         -- previous char
  100                   [Byte],       -- pending bytes on current char
  101                   String)       -- current input string
  102 
  103 ignorePendingBytes :: AlexInput -> AlexInput
  104 ignorePendingBytes (p,c,_ps,s) = (p,c,[],s)
  105 
  106 alexInputPrevChar :: AlexInput -> Char
  107 alexInputPrevChar (_p,c,_bs,_s) = c
  108 
  109 alexGetByte :: AlexInput -> Maybe (Byte,AlexInput)
  110 alexGetByte (p,c,(b:bs),s) = Just (b,(p,c,bs,s))
  111 alexGetByte (_,_,[],[]) = Nothing
  112 alexGetByte (p,_,[],(c:s))  = let p' = alexMove p c
  113                               in case utf8Encode' c of
  114                                    (b, bs) -> p' `seq`  Just (b, (p', c, bs, s))
  115 #endif
  116 
  117 #if defined(ALEX_POSN_BYTESTRING) || defined(ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING)
  118 type AlexInput = (AlexPosn,     -- current position,
  119                   Char,         -- previous char
  120                   ByteString.ByteString,        -- current input string
  121                   Int64)           -- bytes consumed so far
  122 
  123 ignorePendingBytes :: AlexInput -> AlexInput
  124 ignorePendingBytes i = i   -- no pending bytes when lexing bytestrings
  125 
  126 alexInputPrevChar :: AlexInput -> Char
  127 alexInputPrevChar (_,c,_,_) = c
  128 
  129 alexGetByte :: AlexInput -> Maybe (Byte,AlexInput)
  130 alexGetByte (p,_,cs,n) =
  131     case ByteString.uncons cs of
  132         Nothing -> Nothing
  133         Just (b, cs') ->
  134             let c   = ByteString.w2c b
  135                 p'  = alexMove p c
  136                 n'  = n+1
  137             in p' `seq` cs' `seq` n' `seq` Just (b, (p', c, cs',n'))
  138 #endif
  139 
  140 #ifdef ALEX_BASIC_BYTESTRING
  141 data AlexInput = AlexInput { alexChar :: {-# UNPACK #-} !Char,      -- previous char
  142                              alexStr ::  !ByteString.ByteString,    -- current input string
  143                              alexBytePos :: {-# UNPACK #-} !Int64}  -- bytes consumed so far
  144 
  145 alexInputPrevChar :: AlexInput -> Char
  146 alexInputPrevChar = alexChar
  147 
  148 alexGetByte (AlexInput {alexStr=cs,alexBytePos=n}) =
  149     case ByteString.uncons cs of
  150         Nothing -> Nothing
  151         Just (c, rest) ->
  152             Just (c, AlexInput {
  153                 alexChar = ByteString.w2c c,
  154                 alexStr =  rest,
  155                 alexBytePos = n+1})
  156 #endif
  157 
  158 #ifdef ALEX_STRICT_BYTESTRING
  159 data AlexInput = AlexInput { alexChar :: {-# UNPACK #-} !Char,
  160                              alexStr :: {-# UNPACK #-} !ByteString.ByteString,
  161                              alexBytePos :: {-# UNPACK #-} !Int}
  162 
  163 alexInputPrevChar :: AlexInput -> Char
  164 alexInputPrevChar = alexChar
  165 
  166 alexGetByte (AlexInput {alexStr=cs,alexBytePos=n}) =
  167     case ByteString.uncons cs of
  168         Nothing -> Nothing
  169         Just (c, rest) ->
  170             Just (c, AlexInput {
  171                 alexChar = ByteString.w2c c,
  172                 alexStr =  rest,
  173                 alexBytePos = n+1})
  174 #endif

  176 -- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  177 -- Token positions
  178 
  179 -- `Posn' records the location of a token in the input text.  It has three
  180 -- fields: the address (number of chacaters preceding the token), line number
  181 -- and column of a token within the file. `start_pos' gives the position of the
  182 -- start of the file and `eof_pos' a standard encoding for the end of file.
  183 -- `move_pos' calculates the new position after traversing a given character,
  184 -- assuming the usual eight character tab stops.
  185 
  186 #if defined(ALEX_POSN) || defined(ALEX_MONAD) || defined(ALEX_POSN_BYTESTRING) || defined(ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING) || defined(ALEX_GSCAN)
  187 data AlexPosn = AlexPn !Int !Int !Int
  188         deriving (Eq,Show)
  189 
  190 alexStartPos :: AlexPosn
  191 alexStartPos = AlexPn 0 1 1
  192 
  193 alexMove :: AlexPosn -> Char -> AlexPosn
  194 alexMove (AlexPn a l c) '\t' = AlexPn (a+1)  l     (c+alex_tab_size-((c-1) `mod` alex_tab_size))
  195 alexMove (AlexPn a l _) '\n' = AlexPn (a+1) (l+1)   1
  196 alexMove (AlexPn a l c) _    = AlexPn (a+1)  l     (c+1)
  197 #endif
  198 
  199 -- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  200 -- Monad (default and with ByteString input)
  201 
  202 #if defined(ALEX_MONAD) || defined(ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING)
  203 data AlexState = AlexState {
  204         alex_pos :: !AlexPosn,  -- position at current input location
  205 #ifndef ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING
  206         alex_inp :: String,     -- the current input
  207         alex_chr :: !Char,      -- the character before the input
  208         alex_bytes :: [Byte],
  209 #else /* ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING */
  210         alex_bpos:: !Int64,     -- bytes consumed so far
  211         alex_inp :: ByteString.ByteString,      -- the current input
  212         alex_chr :: !Char,      -- the character before the input
  213 #endif /* ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING */
  214         alex_scd :: !Int        -- the current startcode
  215 #ifdef ALEX_MONAD_USER_STATE
  216       , alex_ust :: AlexUserState -- AlexUserState will be defined in the user program
  217 #endif
  218     }
  219 
  220 -- Compile with -funbox-strict-fields for best results!
  221 
  222 #ifndef ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING
  223 runAlex :: String -> Alex a -> Either String a
  224 runAlex input__ (Alex f)
  225    = case f (AlexState {alex_bytes = [],
  226 #else /* ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING */
  227 runAlex :: ByteString.ByteString -> Alex a -> Either String a
  228 runAlex input__ (Alex f)
  229    = case f (AlexState {alex_bpos = 0,
  230 #endif /* ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING */
  231                         alex_pos = alexStartPos,
  232                         alex_inp = input__,
  233                         alex_chr = '\n',
  234 #ifdef ALEX_MONAD_USER_STATE
  235                         alex_ust = alexInitUserState,
  236 #endif
  237                         alex_scd = 0}) of Left msg -> Left msg
  238                                           Right ( _, a ) -> Right a
  239 
  240 newtype Alex a = Alex { unAlex :: AlexState -> Either String (AlexState, a) }
  241 
  242 instance Functor Alex where
  243   fmap f a = Alex $ \s -> case unAlex a s of
  244                             Left msg -> Left msg
  245                             Right (s', a') -> Right (s', f a')
  246 
  247 instance Applicative Alex where
  248   pure a   = Alex $ \s -> Right (s, a)
  249   fa <*> a = Alex $ \s -> case unAlex fa s of
  250                             Left msg -> Left msg
  251                             Right (s', f) -> case unAlex a s' of
  252                                                Left msg -> Left msg
  253                                                Right (s'', b) -> Right (s'', f b)
  254 
  255 instance Monad Alex where
  256   m >>= k  = Alex $ \s -> case unAlex m s of
  257                                 Left msg -> Left msg
  258                                 Right (s',a) -> unAlex (k a) s'
  259   return = App.pure
  260 
  261 alexGetInput :: Alex AlexInput
  262 alexGetInput
  263 #ifndef ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING
  264  = Alex $ \s@AlexState{alex_pos=pos,alex_chr=c,alex_bytes=bs,alex_inp=inp__} ->
  265         Right (s, (pos,c,bs,inp__))
  266 #else /* ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING */
  267  = Alex $ \s@AlexState{alex_pos=pos,alex_bpos=bpos,alex_chr=c,alex_inp=inp__} ->
  268         Right (s, (pos,c,inp__,bpos))
  269 #endif /* ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING */
  270 
  271 alexSetInput :: AlexInput -> Alex ()
  272 #ifndef ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING
  273 alexSetInput (pos,c,bs,inp__)
  274  = Alex $ \s -> case s{alex_pos=pos,alex_chr=c,alex_bytes=bs,alex_inp=inp__} of
  275 #else /* ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING */
  276 alexSetInput (pos,c,inp__,bpos)
  277  = Alex $ \s -> case s{alex_pos=pos,
  278                        alex_bpos=bpos,
  279                        alex_chr=c,
  280                        alex_inp=inp__} of
  281 #endif /* ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING */
  282                   state__@(AlexState{}) -> Right (state__, ())
  283 
  284 alexError :: String -> Alex a
  285 alexError message = Alex $ const $ Left message
  286 
  287 alexGetStartCode :: Alex Int
  288 alexGetStartCode = Alex $ \s@AlexState{alex_scd=sc} -> Right (s, sc)
  289 
  290 alexSetStartCode :: Int -> Alex ()
  291 alexSetStartCode sc = Alex $ \s -> Right (s{alex_scd=sc}, ())
  292 
  293 #if !defined(ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING) && defined(ALEX_MONAD_USER_STATE)
  294 alexGetUserState :: Alex AlexUserState
  295 alexGetUserState = Alex $ \s@AlexState{alex_ust=ust} -> Right (s,ust)
  296 
  297 alexSetUserState :: AlexUserState -> Alex ()
  298 alexSetUserState ss = Alex $ \s -> Right (s{alex_ust=ss}, ())
  299 #endif /* !defined(ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING) && defined(ALEX_MONAD_USER_STATE) */
  300 
  301 alexMonadScan = do
  302 #ifndef ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING
  303   inp__ <- alexGetInput
  304 #else /* ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING */
  305   inp__@(_,_,_,n) <- alexGetInput
  306 #endif /* ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING */
  307   sc <- alexGetStartCode
  308   case alexScan inp__ sc of
  309     AlexEOF -> alexEOF
  310     AlexError ((AlexPn _ line column),_,_,_) -> alexError $ "lexical error at line " ++ (show line) ++ ", column " ++ (show column)
  311     AlexSkip  inp__' _len -> do
  312         alexSetInput inp__'
  313         alexMonadScan
  314 #ifndef ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING
  315     AlexToken inp__' len action -> do
  316 #else /* ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING */
  317     AlexToken inp__'@(_,_,_,n') _ action -> let len = n'-n in do
  318 #endif /* ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING */
  319         alexSetInput inp__'
  320         action (ignorePendingBytes inp__) len
  321 
  322 -- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  323 -- Useful token actions
  324 
  325 #ifndef ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING
  326 type AlexAction result = AlexInput -> Int -> Alex result
  327 #else /* ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING */
  328 type AlexAction result = AlexInput -> Int64 -> Alex result
  329 #endif /* ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING */
  330 
  331 -- just ignore this token and scan another one
  332 -- skip :: AlexAction result
  333 skip _input _len = alexMonadScan
  334 
  335 -- ignore this token, but set the start code to a new value
  336 -- begin :: Int -> AlexAction result
  337 begin code _input _len = do alexSetStartCode code; alexMonadScan
  338 
  339 -- perform an action for this token, and set the start code to a new value
  340 andBegin :: AlexAction result -> Int -> AlexAction result
  341 (action `andBegin` code) input__ len = do
  342   alexSetStartCode code
  343   action input__ len
  344 
  345 #ifndef ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING
  346 token :: (AlexInput -> Int -> token) -> AlexAction token
  347 #else /* ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING */
  348 token :: (AlexInput -> Int64 -> token) -> AlexAction token
  349 #endif /* ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING */
  350 token t input__ len = return (t input__ len)
  351 #endif /* defined(ALEX_MONAD) || defined(ALEX_MONAD_BYTESTRING) */
  352 
  353 
  354 -- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  355 -- Basic wrapper
  356 
  357 #ifdef ALEX_BASIC
  358 type AlexInput = (Char,[Byte],String)
  359 
  360 alexInputPrevChar :: AlexInput -> Char
  361 alexInputPrevChar (c,_,_) = c
  362 
  363 -- alexScanTokens :: String -> [token]
  364 alexScanTokens str = go ('\n',[],str)
  365   where go inp__@(_,_bs,s) =
  366           case alexScan inp__ 0 of
  367                 AlexEOF -> []
  368                 AlexError _ -> error "lexical error"
  369                 AlexSkip  inp__' _ln     -> go inp__'
  370                 AlexToken inp__' len act -> act (take len s) : go inp__'
  371 
  372 alexGetByte :: AlexInput -> Maybe (Byte,AlexInput)
  373 alexGetByte (c,(b:bs),s) = Just (b,(c,bs,s))
  374 alexGetByte (_,[],[])    = Nothing
  375 alexGetByte (_,[],(c:s)) = case utf8Encode' c of
  376                              (b, bs) -> Just (b, (c, bs, s))
  377 #endif
  378

  380 -- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  381 -- Basic wrapper, ByteString version
  382 
  383 #ifdef ALEX_BASIC_BYTESTRING
  384 
  385 -- alexScanTokens :: ByteString.ByteString -> [token]
  386 alexScanTokens str = go (AlexInput '\n' str 0)
  387   where go inp__ =
  388           case alexScan inp__ 0 of
  389                 AlexEOF -> []
  390                 AlexError _ -> error "lexical error"
  391                 AlexSkip  inp__' _len  -> go inp__'
  392                 AlexToken inp__' _ act ->
  393                   let len = alexBytePos inp__' - alexBytePos inp__ in
  394                   act (ByteString.take len (alexStr inp__)) : go inp__'
  395 
  396 #endif
  397 
  398 #ifdef ALEX_STRICT_BYTESTRING
  399 
  400 -- alexScanTokens :: ByteString.ByteString -> [token]
  401 alexScanTokens str = go (AlexInput '\n' str 0)
  402   where go inp__ =
  403           case alexScan inp__ 0 of
  404                 AlexEOF -> []
  405                 AlexError _ -> error "lexical error"
  406                 AlexSkip  inp__' _len  -> go inp__'
  407                 AlexToken inp__' _ act ->
  408                   let len = alexBytePos inp__' - alexBytePos inp__ in
  409                   act (ByteString.take len (alexStr inp__)) : go inp__'
  410 
  411 #endif
  412

  414 -- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  415 -- Posn wrapper
  416 
  417 -- Adds text positions to the basic model.
  418 
  419 #ifdef ALEX_POSN
  420 --alexScanTokens :: String -> [token]
  421 alexScanTokens str0 = go (alexStartPos,'\n',[],str0)
  422   where go inp__@(pos,_,_,str) =
  423           case alexScan inp__ 0 of
  424                 AlexEOF -> []
  425                 AlexError ((AlexPn _ line column),_,_,_) -> error $ "lexical error at line " ++ (show line) ++ ", column " ++ (show column)
  426                 AlexSkip  inp__' _ln     -> go inp__'
  427                 AlexToken inp__' len act -> act pos (take len str) : go inp__'
  428 #endif
  429 
  430 
  431 -- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  432 -- Posn wrapper, ByteString version
  433 
  434 #ifdef ALEX_POSN_BYTESTRING
  435 --alexScanTokens :: ByteString.ByteString -> [token]
  436 alexScanTokens str0 = go (alexStartPos,'\n',str0,0)
  437   where go inp__@(pos,_,str,n) =
  438           case alexScan inp__ 0 of
  439                 AlexEOF -> []
  440                 AlexError ((AlexPn _ line column),_,_,_) -> error $ "lexical error at line " ++ (show line) ++ ", column " ++ (show column)
  441                 AlexSkip  inp__' _len       -> go inp__'
  442                 AlexToken inp__'@(_,_,_,n') _ act ->
  443                   act pos (ByteString.take (n'-n) str) : go inp__'
  444 #endif

  447 -- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  448 -- GScan wrapper
  449 
  450 -- For compatibility with previous versions of Alex, and because we can.
  451 
  452 #ifdef ALEX_GSCAN
  453 alexGScan stop__ state__ inp__ =
  454   alex_gscan stop__ alexStartPos '\n' [] inp__ (0,state__)
  455 
  456 alex_gscan stop__ p c bs inp__ (sc,state__) =
  457   case alexScan (p,c,bs,inp__) sc of
  458         AlexEOF     -> stop__ p c inp__ (sc,state__)
  459         AlexError _ -> stop__ p c inp__ (sc,state__)
  460         AlexSkip (p',c',bs',inp__') _len ->
  461           alex_gscan stop__ p' c' bs' inp__' (sc,state__)
  462         AlexToken (p',c',bs',inp__') len k ->
  463            k p c inp__ len (\scs -> alex_gscan stop__ p' c' bs' inp__' scs)                  (sc,state__)
  464 #endif
  465 alex_tab_size :: Int
  466 alex_tab_size = 8
  467 alex_base :: AlexAddr

  861 alex_scan_tkn :: AlexUserState -> AlexInput -> Int# -> AlexInput -> Int# -> AlexLastAcc -> (AlexLastAcc, AlexInput)
  862 alexScanUser :: AlexUserState -> AlexInput -> Int -> AlexReturn (AlexInput -> Int -> Alex (Token))
  863 alexScan :: AlexInput -> Int -> AlexReturn (AlexInput -> Int -> Alex (Token))
  864 alexMonadScan :: Alex (Token)
  865 {-# LINE 110 "src/Lexer/Lexer.x" #-}
  866 -- User state to hold comment depth, scanned chars of
  867 -- a string and the position of the read token
  868 data AlexUserState = AlexUserState { commentDepth :: Int
  869                                    , tokenPosn :: AlexPosn
  870                                    , readChars :: [Char]
  871                                    }
  872 
  873 alexInitUserState :: AlexUserState
  874 alexInitUserState = AlexUserState {commentDepth = 0, tokenPosn = AlexPn 0 0 0, readChars = []}
  875 
  876 getCommentDepth :: Alex Int
  877 getCommentDepth = commentDepth <$> alexGetUserState
  878 
  879 setCommentDepth :: Int -> Alex ()
  880 setCommentDepth d = do
  881   state <- alexGetUserState
  882   alexSetUserState $ state{commentDepth = d}
  883 
  884 tokenPosnOfAlexState :: AlexState -> AlexPosn
  885 tokenPosnOfAlexState = tokenPosn . alex_ust
  886 
  887 setTokenPosn :: AlexPosn -> Alex ()
  888 setTokenPosn p = do
  889   state <- alexGetUserState
  890   alexSetUserState $ state{tokenPosn = p}
  891 
  892 getReadChars :: Alex [Char]
  893 getReadChars = readChars <$> alexGetUserState
  894 
  895 setReadChars :: [Char] -> Alex ()
  896 setReadChars r = do
  897   state <- alexGetUserState
  898   alexSetUserState $ state{readChars = r}
  899 
  900 appendChar :: Char -> Alex ()
  901 appendChar c = do
  902   state <- alexGetUserState
  903   alexSetUserState $ state{readChars = c : readChars state}
  904 
  905 -- Utils for position
  906 getLineOfPosn :: AlexPosn -> Int
  907 getLineOfPosn (AlexPn _ line _) = line
  908 
  909 getColumnOfPosn :: AlexPosn -> Int
  910 getColumnOfPosn (AlexPn _ _ col) = col
  911 
  912 printPosn :: AlexPosn -> String
  913 printPosn posn = "line: " ++ show (getLineOfPosn posn) ++
  914             " and column: " ++ show (getColumnOfPosn posn)
  915 
  916 -- Error handling utils
  917 lexicalError :: AlexPosn -> String -> Alex a
  918 lexicalError posn message = alexError $ position ++ message where
  919   position = "Error at " ++ printPosn posn ++ ". "
  920 
  921 unknownCharacter :: AlexAction Token
  922 unknownCharacter (posn, _, _, current_string) len =
  923   let lexeme = (take len current_string)
  924   in lexicalError posn ("Unknown character: " ++ lexeme)
  925 
  926 -- Handle end of file
  927 alexEOF :: Alex Token
  928 alexEOF = do
  929   code <- alexGetStartCode
  930   if code == comment
  931     then alexError "Reached end of file without closing all comments"
  932     else case code of
  933       0 -> return T_eof
  934       c -> alexError $ "Reached end of file in unsupported start code: " ++ show c
  935 
  936 -- Utils for handling tokens
  937 keyword :: Token -> AlexAction Token
  938 keyword tokenConstr (posn, _, _, _) _ = do
  939   setTokenPosn posn
  940   return tokenConstr
  941 
  942 identifiersAction :: (String -> Token) -> AlexAction Token
  943 identifiersAction tokenConstr (posn, _, _, current_string) len = do
  944   setTokenPosn posn
  945   return $ tokenConstr (take len current_string)
  946 
  947 intAction :: AlexAction Token
  948 intAction (posn, _, _, current_string) len =
  949   let lexeme = (take len current_string)
  950   in case readMaybe lexeme :: Maybe Int of
  951     Just v  -> do
  952       setTokenPosn posn
  953       return (T_const_int v)
  954     Nothing -> lexicalError posn ("Unable to parse: " ++ lexeme ++ " into an int")
  955 
  956 floatAction :: AlexAction Token
  957 floatAction (posn, _, _, current_string) len =
  958   let lexeme = (take len current_string)
  959   in case readMaybe lexeme :: Maybe Float of
  960     Just v  -> do
  961       setTokenPosn posn
  962       return (T_const_float v)
  963     Nothing -> lexicalError posn ("Unable to parse: " ++ lexeme ++ " into a float")
  964 
  965 -- Char/String handling
  966 beginString :: AlexAction Token
  967 beginString input@(posn, _, _, _) len = do
  968   code <- alexGetStartCode
  969   case code of
  970     0 -> do
  971       alexSetStartCode string
  972       setReadChars ""
  973       setTokenPosn posn
  974     c -> lexicalError posn ("Unexpected startCode: " ++ show c ++ " in beginString")
  975   skip input len
  976 
  977 endString :: AlexAction Token
  978 endString (posn, _, _, _) _ = do
  979   code <- alexGetStartCode
  980   if code == string
  981     then do
  982       alexSetStartCode 0
  983       chars <- reverse <$> getReadChars
  984       return (T_const_string chars)
  985     else lexicalError posn ("Unexpected startCode: " ++ show code ++ " in endString")
  986 
  987 stringAction :: AlexAction Token
  988 stringAction input@(posn, _, _, current_string) len =
  989   let lexeme = (take len current_string)
  990       finalChar = parseCharLexeme lexeme
  991   in case finalChar of
  992       Just c  -> do
  993         appendChar c
  994         skip input len
  995       Nothing -> lexicalError posn ("Unable to parse: " ++ lexeme ++ " into a char of a string")
  996 
  997 charAction :: AlexAction Token
  998 charAction (posn, _, _, current_string) len =
  999   let lexeme = (take len current_string)
 1000       finalChar = removeFromHead '\'' lexeme >>= removeFromTail '\'' >>= parseCharLexeme
 1001   in case finalChar :: Maybe Char of
 1002     Just ch -> do
 1003       setTokenPosn posn
 1004       return (T_const_char ch)
 1005     _ -> lexicalError posn ("Unable to parse: " ++ lexeme ++ " into a char")
 1006 
 1007 -- Utils for chars
 1008 removeFromHead :: (Eq a) => a -> [a] -> Maybe [a]
 1009 removeFromHead _ [] = Nothing
 1010 removeFromHead a (x:xs) | a == x    = Just xs
 1011                         | otherwise = Nothing
 1012 
 1013 removeFromTail :: (Eq a) => a -> [a] -> Maybe [a]
 1014 removeFromTail _ []     = Nothing
 1015 removeFromTail a (x:[]) | a == x    = Just []
 1016                         | otherwise = Nothing
 1017 removeFromTail a (x:xs) = (x:) <$> removeFromTail a xs
 1018 
 1019 hexToInt :: Char -> Maybe Int
 1020 hexToInt c =
 1021   let o = ord c
 1022   in case isHexDigit c of
 1023     False -> Nothing
 1024     True | ord '0' <= o && o <= ord '9' -> Just (o - ord '0')
 1025     True | ord 'A' <= o && o <= ord 'F' -> Just (o - ord 'A')
 1026     True | ord 'a' <= o && o <= ord 'f' -> Just (o - ord 'a')
 1027     _ -> Nothing
 1028 
 1029 parseHex :: String -> Maybe Int
 1030 parseHex = aux (0 :: Integer) . reverse where
 1031   aux _ []     = Nothing
 1032   aux n [c]    = ((16 ^ n) *) <$> hexToInt c
 1033   aux n (c:cs) = do
 1034     cv <- hexToInt c
 1035     csv <- aux (n + 1) cs
 1036     return ((16 ^ n) * cv + csv)
 1037 
 1038 parseCharLexeme :: String -> Maybe Char
 1039 parseCharLexeme s = case s of
 1040   [c]    -> Just c
 1041   "\\n"  -> Just '\n'
 1042   "\\t"  -> Just '\t'
 1043   "\\r"  -> Just '\r'
 1044   "\\0"  -> Just '\0'
 1045   "\\\\" -> Just '\\'
 1046   "\\\'" -> Just '\''
 1047   "\\\"" -> Just '\"'
 1048   '\\':'x':hex1:hex0:"" | ord '0' <= ord hex1 && ord hex1 <= ord '7' -> chr <$> parseHex (hex1:hex0:"")
 1049   _      -> Nothing
 1050 
 1051 -- Comments utils
 1052 beginComment :: AlexAction Token
 1053 beginComment input len = do
 1054   alexSetStartCode comment
 1055   d <- getCommentDepth
 1056   setCommentDepth (d + 1)
 1057   skip input len
 1058 
 1059 endComment :: AlexAction Token
 1060 endComment input@(posn, _, _, _) len = do
 1061   d <- getCommentDepth
 1062   when (d == 1) (alexSetStartCode 0)
 1063   if d > 0
 1064     then setCommentDepth (d - 1)
 1065     else lexicalError posn "A comment closed without being opened"
 1066   skip input len
 1067 
 1068 
 1069 -- Utils for running lexer
 1070 -- Scan a string until EOF is encountered
 1071 lexer :: String -> Either String [Token]
 1072 lexer s = runAlex s gather where
 1073   gather :: Alex [Token]
 1074   gather = do
 1075      t <- alexMonadScan
 1076      case t of
 1077        T_eof -> return [t]
 1078        _     -> (t:) <$> gather
 1079 
 1080 -- Scan a file
 1081 scanFile :: FilePath -> IO (Either String [Token])
 1082 scanFile f = do
 1083   inp <- readFile f
 1084   return $ lexer inp
 1085 
 1086 -- Scan a line
 1087 lexerLine :: IO ()
 1088 lexerLine = do
 1089   line <- getLine
 1090   let res = lexer line
 1091   case res of
 1092     Left err     -> putStrLn err
 1093     Right tokens -> mapM_ (\t -> putStrLn $ "Token: " ++ show t) tokens
 1094 
 1095 comment,string :: Int
 1096 comment = 1
 1097 string = 2

 1350 #ifdef ALEX_GHC
 1351 #  define ILIT(n) n#
 1352 #  define IBOX(n) (I# (n))
 1353 #  define FAST_INT Int#
 1354 -- Do not remove this comment. Required to fix CPP parsing when using GCC and a clang-compiled alex.
 1355 #  if __GLASGOW_HASKELL__ > 706
 1356 #    define GTE(n,m) (tagToEnum# (n >=# m))
 1357 #    define EQ(n,m) (tagToEnum# (n ==# m))
 1358 #  else
 1359 #    define GTE(n,m) (n >=# m)
 1360 #    define EQ(n,m) (n ==# m)
 1361 #  endif
 1362 #  define PLUS(n,m) (n +# m)
 1363 #  define MINUS(n,m) (n -# m)
 1364 #  define TIMES(n,m) (n *# m)
 1365 #  define NEGATE(n) (negateInt# (n))
 1366 #  define IF_GHC(x) (x)
 1367 #else
 1368 #  define ILIT(n) (n)
 1369 #  define IBOX(n) (n)
 1370 #  define FAST_INT Int
 1371 #  define GTE(n,m) (n >= m)
 1372 #  define EQ(n,m) (n == m)
 1373 #  define PLUS(n,m) (n + m)
 1374 #  define MINUS(n,m) (n - m)
 1375 #  define TIMES(n,m) (n * m)
 1376 #  define NEGATE(n) (negate (n))
 1377 #  define IF_GHC(x)
 1378 #endif
 1379 
 1380 #ifdef ALEX_GHC
 1381 data AlexAddr = AlexA# Addr#
 1382 -- Do not remove this comment. Required to fix CPP parsing when using GCC and a clang-compiled alex.
 1383 #if __GLASGOW_HASKELL__ < 503
 1384 uncheckedShiftL# = shiftL#
 1385 #endif
 1386 
 1387 {-# INLINE alexIndexInt16OffAddr #-}
 1388 alexIndexInt16OffAddr :: AlexAddr -> Int# -> Int#
 1389 alexIndexInt16OffAddr (AlexA# arr) off =
 1390 #ifdef WORDS_BIGENDIAN
 1391   narrow16Int# i
 1392   where
 1393         i    = word2Int# ((high `uncheckedShiftL#` 8#) `or#` low)
 1394         high = int2Word# (ord# (indexCharOffAddr# arr (off' +# 1#)))
 1395         low  = int2Word# (ord# (indexCharOffAddr# arr off'))
 1396         off' = off *# 2#
 1397 #else
 1398 #if __GLASGOW_HASKELL__ >= 901
 1399   int16ToInt#
 1400 #endif
 1401     (indexInt16OffAddr# arr off)
 1402 #endif
 1403 #else
 1404 alexIndexInt16OffAddr arr off = arr ! off
 1405 #endif
 1406 
 1407 #ifdef ALEX_GHC
 1408 {-# INLINE alexIndexInt32OffAddr #-}
 1409 alexIndexInt32OffAddr :: AlexAddr -> Int# -> Int#
 1410 alexIndexInt32OffAddr (AlexA# arr) off =
 1411 #ifdef WORDS_BIGENDIAN
 1412   narrow32Int# i
 1413   where
 1414    i    = word2Int# ((b3 `uncheckedShiftL#` 24#) `or#`
 1415                      (b2 `uncheckedShiftL#` 16#) `or#`
 1416                      (b1 `uncheckedShiftL#` 8#) `or#` b0)
 1417    b3   = int2Word# (ord# (indexCharOffAddr# arr (off' +# 3#)))
 1418    b2   = int2Word# (ord# (indexCharOffAddr# arr (off' +# 2#)))
 1419    b1   = int2Word# (ord# (indexCharOffAddr# arr (off' +# 1#)))
 1420    b0   = int2Word# (ord# (indexCharOffAddr# arr off'))
 1421    off' = off *# 4#
 1422 #else
 1423 #if __GLASGOW_HASKELL__ >= 901
 1424   int32ToInt#
 1425 #endif
 1426     (indexInt32OffAddr# arr off)
 1427 #endif
 1428 #else
 1429 alexIndexInt32OffAddr arr off = arr ! off
 1430 #endif
 1431 
 1432 #ifdef ALEX_GHC
 1433 
 1434 #if __GLASGOW_HASKELL__ < 503
 1435 quickIndex arr i = arr ! i
 1436 #else
 1437 -- GHC >= 503, unsafeAt is available from Data.Array.Base.
 1438 quickIndex = unsafeAt
 1439 #endif
 1440 #else
 1441 quickIndex arr i = arr ! i
 1442 #endif
 1443 
 1444 -- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 1445 -- Main lexing routines
 1446 
 1447 data AlexReturn a
 1448   = AlexEOF
 1449   | AlexError  !AlexInput
 1450   | AlexSkip   !AlexInput !Int
 1451   | AlexToken  !AlexInput !Int a
 1452 
 1453 -- alexScan :: AlexInput -> StartCode -> AlexReturn a
 1454 alexScan input__ IBOX(sc)
 1455   = alexScanUser undefined input__ IBOX(sc)
 1456 
 1457 alexScanUser user__ input__ IBOX(sc)
 1458   = case alex_scan_tkn user__ input__ ILIT(0) input__ sc AlexNone of
 1459   (AlexNone, input__') ->
 1460     case alexGetByte input__ of
 1461       Nothing ->
 1462 #ifdef ALEX_DEBUG
 1463                                    trace ("End of input.") $
 1464 #endif
 1465                                    AlexEOF
 1466       Just _ ->
 1467 #ifdef ALEX_DEBUG
 1468                                    trace ("Error.") $
 1469 #endif
 1470                                    AlexError input__'
 1471 
 1472   (AlexLastSkip input__'' len, _) ->
 1473 #ifdef ALEX_DEBUG
 1474     trace ("Skipping.") $
 1475 #endif
 1476     AlexSkip input__'' len
 1477 
 1478   (AlexLastAcc k input__''' len, _) ->
 1479 #ifdef ALEX_DEBUG
 1480     trace ("Accept.") $
 1481 #endif
 1482     AlexToken input__''' len (alex_actions ! k)
 1483 
 1484 
 1485 -- Push the input through the DFA, remembering the most recent accepting
 1486 -- state it encountered.
 1487 
 1488 alex_scan_tkn user__ orig_input len input__ s last_acc =
 1489   input__ `seq` -- strict in the input
 1490   let
 1491   new_acc = (check_accs (alex_accept `quickIndex` IBOX(s)))
 1492   in
 1493   new_acc `seq`
 1494   case alexGetByte input__ of
 1495      Nothing -> (new_acc, input__)
 1496      Just (c, new_input) ->
 1497 #ifdef ALEX_DEBUG
 1498       trace ("State: " ++ show IBOX(s) ++ ", char: " ++ show c) $
 1499 #endif
 1500       case fromIntegral c of { IBOX(ord_c) ->
 1501         let
 1502                 base   = alexIndexInt32OffAddr alex_base s
 1503                 offset = PLUS(base,ord_c)
 1504                 check  = alexIndexInt16OffAddr alex_check offset
 1505 
 1506                 new_s = if GTE(offset,ILIT(0)) && EQ(check,ord_c)
 1507                           then alexIndexInt16OffAddr alex_table offset
 1508                           else alexIndexInt16OffAddr alex_deflt s
 1509         in
 1510         case new_s of
 1511             ILIT(-1) -> (new_acc, input__)
 1512                 -- on an error, we want to keep the input *before* the
 1513                 -- character that failed, not after.
 1514             _ -> alex_scan_tkn user__ orig_input
 1515 #ifdef ALEX_LATIN1
 1516                    PLUS(len,ILIT(1))
 1517                    -- issue 119: in the latin1 encoding, *each* byte is one character
 1518 #else
 1519                    (if c < 0x80 || c >= 0xC0 then PLUS(len,ILIT(1)) else len)
 1520                    -- note that the length is increased ONLY if this is the 1st byte in a char encoding)
 1521 #endif
 1522                    new_input new_s new_acc
 1523       }
 1524   where
 1525         check_accs (AlexAccNone) = last_acc
 1526         check_accs (AlexAcc a  ) = AlexLastAcc a input__ IBOX(len)
 1527         check_accs (AlexAccSkip) = AlexLastSkip  input__ IBOX(len)
 1528 #ifndef ALEX_NOPRED
 1529         check_accs (AlexAccPred a predx rest)
 1530            | predx user__ orig_input IBOX(len) input__
 1531            = AlexLastAcc a input__ IBOX(len)
 1532            | otherwise
 1533            = check_accs rest
 1534         check_accs (AlexAccSkipPred predx rest)
 1535            | predx user__ orig_input IBOX(len) input__
 1536            = AlexLastSkip input__ IBOX(len)
 1537            | otherwise
 1538            = check_accs rest
 1539 #endif
 1540 
 1541 data AlexLastAcc
 1542   = AlexNone
 1543   | AlexLastAcc !Int !AlexInput !Int
 1544   | AlexLastSkip     !AlexInput !Int
 1545 
 1546 data AlexAcc user
 1547   = AlexAccNone
 1548   | AlexAcc Int
 1549   | AlexAccSkip
 1550 #ifndef ALEX_NOPRED
 1551   | AlexAccPred Int (AlexAccPred user) (AlexAcc user)
 1552   | AlexAccSkipPred (AlexAccPred user) (AlexAcc user)
 1553 
 1554 type AlexAccPred user = user -> AlexInput -> Int -> AlexInput -> Bool
 1555 
 1556 -- -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
 1557 -- Predicates on a rule
 1558 
 1559 alexAndPred p1 p2 user__ in1 len in2
 1560   = p1 user__ in1 len in2 && p2 user__ in1 len in2
 1561 
 1562 --alexPrevCharIsPred :: Char -> AlexAccPred _
 1563 alexPrevCharIs c _ input__ _ _ = c == alexInputPrevChar input__
 1564 
 1565 alexPrevCharMatches f _ input__ _ _ = f (alexInputPrevChar input__)
 1566 
 1567 --alexPrevCharIsOneOfPred :: Array Char Bool -> AlexAccPred _
 1568 alexPrevCharIsOneOf arr _ input__ _ _ = arr ! alexInputPrevChar input__
 1569 
 1570 --alexRightContext :: Int -> AlexAccPred _
 1571 alexRightContext IBOX(sc) user__ _ _ input__ =
 1572      case alex_scan_tkn user__ input__ ILIT(0) input__ sc AlexNone of
 1573           (AlexNone, _) -> False
 1574           _ -> True
 1575         -- TODO: there's no need to find the longest
 1576         -- match when checking the right context, just
 1577         -- the first match will do.
 1578 #endif
